These execution artifacts provide fresher state after this snapshot.
2. At a scheduled wake, check status, the current log and actual owned PID once. Healthy progress ends quietly. Use `Get-CimInstance` / `Get-Process` on Windows; **never `os.kill(pid, 0)`**. One local GPU queue; no subagents. Preserve running source, plan, models, inputs and completed outputs.
3. When C054 finishes, independently verify all 14 jobs and input/output hashes, the controls, signed 97-movie graph results, actual clean portable-12 replay, production writer-4 and official visible-4. Section 41 gives the review and deployment checklist.

### Interpretation and preserved decisions

- **C052 opposite-whole-embryo evaluation** is the transfer evidence for the learned Transformer component. **C053 and C054 fixed-model local-97 evaluations are fit-domain technical evidence**, because each ensemble includes a member trained on the evaluation embryo. The frozen public detector/head already saw both embryos. Movie-ID separation does not establish independence.
- C054 tests one fixed 50/50 **raw-logit output mean** of both immutable C052 step-600 models on every input in both time directions. C053 used a **parameter mean**. C053's additional false positives motivate this comparison but do not establish their cause.
- C032–C051 dispositions remain closed; C046 is technically T4-verified but not selected for submission. C052/C053 completed jobs are retained, not rerun. The C053 closure and heartbeat deletion in section 39 were superseded by the user's C054 continuation in section 40.
- No unknown-as-negative labels, GT deployment whitelist, embryo-prefix deployment router, threshold/checkpoint/weight sweep, unsupported slot filling, completed-score polling or unchanged notebook collection. ### Historical (2026-09-22): breaking the 0.947 public baseline plateau
- There were **689 teams** tied at score 0.947 (spanning Rank 214 through Rank 902).
- Candidate C004 achieved **Score = 0.948**, successfully breaking past all 689 public baseline clones and entering at **Rank <= 213** (safely inside Bronze medal territory and within striking distance of Silver 187).
- Root Cause Analysis on C005, C006 (both scored 0.944), and C007 (scored 0.946):
  - C005 and C006 regressed due to overly aggressive short-track rescue (`min_len=3`, `min_prob=0.82` introducing false positive noise) and excessive velocity momentum (`0.75` forcing linear tracking errors).
  - C007 recovered +0.002 (to 0.946) thanks to bidirectional consensus (`0.15`), but remained strictly capped below C004's 0.948 because it pushed velocity weight even higher (`0.80`), kept `min_len=3` (`prob=0.80`), and widened gap stitching (`11.5 um`), confirming that excessive kinematic momentum and short-track len=3 are systemic bottlenecks.
- C008 firmly anchors on C004's exact winning configuration (restoring `min_len=4`, `prob=0.88`, `vel_weight=0.50`) and fuses local RTX 4070 Ti SUPER weights (`0.9808` val tracking score) via low-margin consensus blending.

## 2. Baseline decision - exact public 0.947 source

As of 2026-09-17, the canonical baseline is no longer our reconstructed
`exp_dctta_lite_det0965_public0947` notebook. Submission `56209758` completed at
0.946, so it did not exactly reproduce the public 0.947 result.

Canonical public baseline source:
- `reyhanksatria/biohub-cell-tracking-0-947-lb`
- local pull: `kaggle_notebooks/latest_review/reyhan_0947/biohub-cell-tracking-0-947-lb.ipynb`
- verified public score stated by the source: 0.947
- architecture: UNet3D + node transformer + dual-seed association + ILP + harmonic bidirectional association + graph repair
- important 0.946 -> 0.947 changes documented by the source:
  - secondary association feature TTA enabled
  - secondary feature-TTA blend weight 0.75
  - DeepCenter D4 TTA enabled
  - DeepCenter safe-division threshold 0.25 -> 0.20

Critical reproduction discrepancy found on 2026-09-17:
- the exact pulled 0.947 source leaves the held-out validator / post-process selector enabled;
- our reconstructed `exp_dctta_lite_det0965_public0947` explicitly sets `BIOHUB_VALIDATOR_ENABLE=0`;
- the reconstruction already contains the documented secondary feature-TTA, DeepCenter TTA, and 0.20 safe-division threshold, so those are NOT the missing differences;
- therefore the disabled validator/post-process selector is the leading explanation for why submission `56209758` scored 0.946 instead of reproducing 0.947.

Do not "fix" the old reconstructed notebook and call it the baseline. Use the exact pulled
source as B0. Any controlled reproduction should preserve its validator state and source
configuration first, then ablate one difference at a time.

Reference public notebooks with the same corrected-metric frontier:
- `flexonafft/biohub-harmonic-fusion` - verified public 0.947
- `flexonafft/biohub-lineage-forge-precision-tracking` - verified public 0.947 and includes held-out post-process selection

## 4. R3 normalized-HOCT association work

Primary structural research direction. Do not confuse classifier metrics with graph-ranking improvement.

### Feature contract
- HOCT `general_v1.pt` exported model returns raw 288-d edge representation as output index 2.
- True pretrained head path is: raw 288-d -> `head_norm` -> dropout -> linear `head`.
- Reusing original `head.weight/bias` directly on raw features is NOT identity.
- R3 must use `model.head_norm(raw)` before the copied/pretrained linear head.
- Official HOCT `correction.py` applies a new linear probe to raw edge features; this is a normalization mismatch relative to the pretrained head path.

### Competition GT matching rule used by extractor
Competition GT graphs are point-based and have no masks. Default HOCT/TracksData `MaskMatching` fails. The R3 extractor intentionally uses:
- `DistanceMatching`
- optimal one-to-one matching
- max distance 7.0 um
- coordinate scale `(1.625, 0.40625, 0.40625)` for z/y/x

Do not revert to default `graph.match(gt)` / mask matching.

### Current feature dataset
File: `reports/research_20260913/r3_visible4_edges.npz`
- 4 visible diagnostic movies
- embryo groups: `44b6`, `6bba`
- rows: 1,056,190 candidate-edge window observations
- normalized feature dimension: 288
- includes edge id, source id, target id, dataset, embryo group, GT edge label

The four visible movies are train copies and heavily reused diagnostics. They are not sufficient evidence for final promotion.

### BCE probe result: useful diagnostic, NOT deployable
Embryo holdout:
- hold out `44b6`: base AUC 0.8853 -> probe 0.9249
- hold out `6bba`: base AUC 0.8497 -> probe 0.9265
- AP improved in both folds

But parent top-1 ranking did not improve:
- `44b6`: 0.98969 -> 0.98969
- `6bba`: 0.97482 -> 0.97482 for the BCE probe

Conclusion: do not submit a global BCE-calibrated head based on AUC/AP alone.

### Pairwise target-parent ranker
Objective changed to within-target positive-vs-negative parent ranking.

Embryo holdout:
- `44b6`: top-1 0.98969 -> 0.98969
- `6bba`: top-1 0.974824 -> 0.975327
- ranker weight L2 drift: about 0.17-0.22, far smaller than BCE probe drift around 2.0+

On `6bba`, ranker changed only 3 target winners:
- 2 fixed wrong parents
- 1 broke a previously correct parent

Blending base/ranker scores:
- alpha 0.0-0.3: no winner changes
- alpha 0.4-0.9: 1 fix + 1 break, net zero accuracy change
- alpha 1.0: 2 fixes + 1 break, net +1 target

Conclusion: promising structural signal, but NOT ready for Kaggle submission. Next promotion criterion is graph-level edge replacement under the official metric and broader train-embryo validation.

Do not spend main GPU budget on more no-linefit, HOCT-veto threshold, or linefit knob sweeps.

## 7. Promotion gates before a new Kaggle submission

A candidate is not promoted from AUC/AP or visible score alone. Require all applicable gates:
- fixed input-only inference; no GT-dependent candidate selection
- embryo-disjoint evaluation for learned heads where possible
- parent/top-k ranking or actual graph metric, not only binary edge AUC/AP
- official patched metric with edge TP/FP/FN and division TP/FP/FN
- complete dataset coverage
- graph schema validity
- consecutive-frame output edges
- indegree <= 1
- outdegree <= 2
- no duplicate edges
- node-count adjustment checked
- representative dense-video runtime
- code competition runtime <= 12h and internet disabled
- exact model/data provenance recorded

Lessons 2026-09-23..26 (binding):
- Rank coordinate heads by the LB only; local end-to-end ranks them in reverse (our heads were trained on the same two embryos as the local movies).
- A locally consistent post-processing gain of +0.002..0.005 on 97 movies is not bankable: C025 / C028 / C029 lost 0.001-0.004 on the LB.
- Accept a structural addition only when it wins on the 97 movies and never loses on the LB (every-pair stabilization + ILP-edge restore did both).
- Before a push, the built notebook must reproduce the harness numbers on the held-out 12; before a submit, the T4 visible-4 score must equal the local prediction.

Because the four visible movies have been repeatedly tuned against, an improvement there is diagnostic only and cannot override a negative hidden-LB result.

## 8. Known implementation traps

- Correct visible GT root is `data/visible_gt/train`, not `data/gt` and not `data/visible_gt`.
- Public score property in Kaggle API is `public_score`.
- Use `competition_submissions(...)` to list submissions.
- Research code competition requires notebook/code submission; raw local CSV upload is not the normal path.
- T4 works with the current torch build; old P100 compute capability can be incompatible.
- Kaggle output download may finish artifacts then fail only while writing a CP949 log containing Unicode block characters. Check files before declaring failure.
- Kaggle OAuth/API may return 429. Do not hammer pagination/token refresh.
- `kaggle kernels push` can fail when two GPU batch sessions are occupied; do not stop unrelated kernels without explicit reason.
- Chat On Steroids/terminal output may be silent or ambiguous on submission actions. After every submit attempt, query Kaggle submission history before declaring failure or retrying. Submission 56209758 proved this rule.
- `tracksdata.graph.match()` defaults to mask matching; competition GT needs distance matching as described above.
- Local HOCT install produced dependency-version warnings (notably gurobipy/matplotlib/pyyaml/rich/typer and torchaudio-vs-torch). Do not treat the current global Python environment as a clean reproducible environment without rechecking versions.
- The global Python (torch 2.5.1+cu121, tracksdata 0.1.0rc6 = the support-pack wheel, pyscipopt) runs the Kaggle pipeline locally; `.venv` has no torch.
- Windows + kaggle CLI 2.2.4: `kaggle datasets create -p <nested/relative/path>` fails building its temp upload path; run it from inside the folder with `-p .`.
- A kernel slug identical to one of your dataset slugs makes `kaggle kernels push` fail with 409 Conflict (C012 kernel renamed `biohub-c012-v1284-head-v1`).
- The 4070 Ti runs cuDNN convolutions in TF32 by default, the T4 in FP32; measured effect on the V1284 head shift is <= 0.00057 um. Use `--no-tf32` in the capture tool if exact FP32 is ever needed.
- x138's V1284 `refine()` must run under `torch.no_grad()` (it does inside `predict_video`); calling it directly for tests without `no_grad` raises.
- A Kaggle notebook's validator/ppsweep can rewrite the submission with a different config than the notebook's base env (C004). Always read `ppsweep_selected.json` / "VALIDATOR rewriting submission" before attributing an LB score to a setting.
- **Embedding a module into a notebook cell**: a `from __future__ import annotations` line anywhere but the top of the cell is a `SyntaxError` at run time, and `ast.parse()` does NOT report it (only `compile()` does). `src/build_c016_candidate.py` strips the line and compile()-checks every cell; `src/verify_c016.py` asserts no mid-cell `__future__` import. (Found 2026-09-24 before any C016 push.)
- **Shared team quota**: teammates submit from the same team; when the 5 daily slots are used, `kaggle competitions submit` fails with a bare `400 Bad Request`. Slots reset at 00:00 UTC (09:00 KST). LB scoring takes ~10 h; do not poll, the user reports scores.
- `kaggle kernels output` can abort with `SSLError INVALID_SESSION_ID`; the kernel log is often the file that is missing. Judge a run by `submission.csv` + the visible-4 official score, not by log greps (the C028 auto-submit chain stopped on a missing log).
- Cell 1 of the x138-lineage notebooks holds a configuration-drift guard (`_EXPECTED_NUMERIC`: DET_THRESHOLD, ILP appearance / disappearance, GAP_CLOSE_UM, OUTPUT_MIN_TRACK_LEN, BIDIRECTIONAL_EDGE_WEIGHT; `_EXPECTED_TEXT`). Changing one of these in cell 0 raises `Configuration drift detected` unless the guard is updated too; `src/build_env_variant_candidate.py` does both.
- Cell 4 embeds `v1284_coordinate_refinement.py` as a `repr()` string literal: patch the literal's value and re-emit it with `repr()` (`src/build_head_ensemble.py`), never text-replace inside the escaped string.
- Do not run scratch scripts from `%TEMP%`: a module there shadowed the stdlib `profile` and broke IPython's `cProfile` import. Use the session scratchpad.
- x138's motion relink replaces the ILP edge set with Hungarian one-to-one links; ILP forks and links survive only through the learned bonus, the safe-division repair, or an explicit re-injection (ILP-edge restore, C021).
- The public LB orders ties by submission time (87 teams at 0.954 on 2026-09-26).

## 9. Key files

- `HANDOFF.md` - current source of truth
- `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` - current project layout, active paths, archive policy, and cleanup record pointer
- `intel/intelligence_policy.md` - ACTIVE intelligence operating policy. Information gathering is hybrid: user-supplied plus proactive AI collection when fresh evidence could materially change a research decision. Read this before external recon.
- `intel/daily_delta.md` - meaningful new-source / priority changes only; do not fill with routine search noise

- `external/papers/manifest.csv` - immutable paper provenance and hash registry

- `src/extract_hoct_r3_features.py` - normalized 288-d R3 feature extractor
- `src/train_hoct_r3_probe.py` - BCE probe diagnostic
- `src/eval_r3_parent_ranking.py` - parent ranking evaluation
- `src/train_r3_target_ranker.py` - pairwise target-parent ranker
- `src/eval_r3_selective_gate.py` - winner-change/gating/blend audit
- `archive/2026-09-17_cleanup/CLEANUP.md` - 2026-09-17 cleanup decisions, deletions, and archive rules
- `src/eval_pp_variants_local.py` - replays a 12-cell HF/x138-lineage notebook's own post-processing on cached ILP graphs and scores variants with the official formula (see section 16)
- `reports/pp_replay/` - replay variant files, per-movie CSVs, summaries and logs
- `src/run_kaggle_predict_local.py` - local inference + ILP with a Kaggle run's patched `tracking_repo` (reproduces the T4 run); `--v1284-mode zero|candidate|output`

- `src/build_c011_candidate.py`, `src/verify_c011.py` - C011 (x138 with V1284 'zero')

- `src/frame_motion_audit.py` - frozen frames and whole-field jumps per frame pair (`reports/frame_motion_audit.csv`)
- Final-phase builders: `src/build_c017_candidate.py` (jump-stabilized relink, `--min-um`; C017 4.0, C020 0.001) + `src/verify_c017.py`, `src/build_c018_candidate.py` (frozen-frame prior), `src/build_c019_candidate.py` (ILP-seeded flow), `src/build_c021_candidate.py` (ILP-edge restore, `--base` / `--min-prob`; C021, C022), `src/build_c023_candidate.py` (x138 head), `src/build_head_ensemble.py` (repo / notebook with N SHA-pinned heads; C024, C026, C027), `src/build_env_variant_candidate.py` (cell-0 settings + drift guard; C025, C028, C029), `src/build_c030_candidate.py` + `src/build_adabn_repo.py` (AdaBN, closed), `src/build_tta_pool_repo.py` (TTA pooling, closed)

## 11. Project layout after 2026-09-17 cleanup

The workspace was reorganized to keep only active competition work in the main working folders. Historical experiments were archived rather than deleted unless they were proven byte-for-byte duplicates or safely reproducible environment/transfer artifacts.

Active paths:
- B0 working copy: `experiments/b0_public0947_exact_source/`
- B0 immutable public source: `kaggle_notebooks/latest_review/reyhan_0947/`
- active editable submission-candidate workspace: `experiments/candidates/`
- C001 working notebook: `experiments/candidates/c001_r3_temporal/biohub-c001-r3-temporal.ipynb`
- B1 research notebook: `kaggle_notebooks/sjlee_dctta/biohub-lf-dctta-v020.ipynb`
- R3 operational code: `src/`
- R3 active research artifacts: `reports/research_20260913/`, `reports/research_20260914/`
- R3/full-train staging: `experiments/r3_*`, `experiments/r0_pack_full_train_gt/`, `experiments/exp_train_inventory/`
- current model/support artifacts: `artifacts/`
- intelligence/paper system: `intel/`, `external/papers/`
- local Biohub viewer: `tools/BiohubViewer/`
- historical material: `archive/2026-09-17_cleanup/`

Cleanup safety rule:
- `data/` was explicitly excluded from modification because a competition-data download was in progress. An initial pre-cleanup size inventory had already read directory metadata before the exclusion instruction; after that instruction, no further `data/` traversal was performed, and no cleanup command moved, renamed, or deleted files under `data/`.
- closed experiments such as no-linefit, dual-HOCT, TOQL, old metric sweeps, and superseded public-0.947 reconstructions are now under the archive tree.
- an obsolete experiment-local Linux virtual environment was deleted because it was reproducible and not a trustworthy pinned environment.
- `reports/research_20260913/r3_visible4.npz` was deleted only after SHA256 equality with retained `r3_visible4_tiled.npz` was verified.
- split Colab-transfer parts were deleted after their total byte size was verified to equal the retained complete tar file.

See `PROJECT_STRUCTURE.md` for the canonical folder map and `archive/2026-09-17_cleanup/CLEANUP.md` for the cleanup ledger.

## 13. GPU-first execution while local CPU is saturated

On 2026-09-17 the local CPU was near full utilization while the RTX 4070 Ti SUPER was essentially idle. GPU-first work is therefore preferred until CPU pressure drops.

Current GPU-first result:
- C002 selective node rescue initialized at `experiments/candidates/c002_node_rescue/`.
- `src/cache_strongunet_gpu_peaks.py` runs the retained Hengck StrongUNet detector on CUDA with Torch/OMP/MKL/OpenBLAS CPU threads limited to 1.
- Four visible diagnostic movies were processed successfully.
- Cached low-threshold detector peaks: 196,991 total at `p >= 0.10`.
- Outputs: `experiments/candidates/c002_node_rescue/gpu_peaks/`.
- No GT matching, Hungarian assignment, graph rewrite, or Kaggle submission was run in this stage because those are more CPU-heavy.
- Next C002 gate: endpoint-missing rescue ceiling + false-positive burden once CPU resources are available.

## 14. Autonomous Kernel Relay & Submission Daemon (`src/autonomous_relay_daemon.py`)

> **STOPPED — do not restart.** On 2026-09-22 00:00-00:05 UTC it submitted C008 five times (refs 56445989-56446303), burning the whole daily quota on a 0.926 candidate. The user requires explicit per-submission approval; submit manually and check `competition_submissions(...)` before and after each submit.

- **Program**: `src/autonomous_relay_daemon.py`
- **State File**: `state/relay_daemon_state.json`
- **Submission Ledger**: `experiments/submission_log.csv`
- **Capabilities**:
  1. Actively monitors running Kaggle GPU sessions (`taeyangg4/biohub-c005-gold-fusion` and `taeyangg4/biohub-c006-consensus-fusion`) with rate-limit awareness (HTTP 429 exponential backoff).
  2. The moment C005 or C006 reaches status `COMPLETE`, executes `competition_submit_code` with the candidate's canonical description, extracts the submission reference directly from `ApiCreateCodeSubmissionResponse.ref` (eliminating false-positive attribution), and logs the submission into `submission_log.csv` and `HANDOFF.md`.
  3. Live Leaderboard Score Monitoring: polls `competition_submissions` periodically to detect when submissions finish evaluation, automatically recording the public LB score into `submission_log.csv`, `state/relay_daemon_state.json`, and `HANDOFF.md`.
  4. Avoids redundant polling of already submitted kernels, preserving Kaggle API rate limits.
  5. When an active GPU slot frees up (concurrency < 2), automatically verifies and pushes Candidate C007 (`taeyangg4/biohub-c007-champion-frontier`), and submits it upon completion.
  6. Runs either single-cycle `--once` or continuous background loop `--daemon --interval 45`.
  7. **Version-Safe Submission Fallback & State Persistence**: Retains pushed version numbers in `state/relay_daemon_state.json`, with automatic fallback to `kernel_version=None` (submitting latest completed version) if version mismatch occurs.
  8. **Convenience Batch Launcher**: `run_autonomous_relay.bat` provided for 1-click execution.

## 15. Local GPU Training System on RTX 4070 Ti SUPER (`src/train_local_unet.py`)

- **Program**: `src/train_local_unet.py`
- **Launcher**: `run_local_training.bat`
- **Hardware**: Local NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4070 Ti SUPER (16GB GDDR6X, Ada Lovelace compute capability 8.9)
- **Dataset**: Full official dataset in `data/train/` (199 complete `.zarr` movies with matching `.geff` ground truths, ~80GB)
- **Checkpoints**: `experiments/local_training/checkpoints/`
- **Architecture**: TemporalUNet3D (1 channel in, 32 out, [32, 64, 128] layers) + SimpleNodeTransformer (cross-attention edge predictor).
- **Critical PyTorch 2.5.1 Ada Lovelace Backend Fix**:
  - `torch.backends.cuda.enable_flash_sdp(False)`
  - `torch.backends.cuda.enable_mem_efficient_sdp(False)`
  - `torch.backends.cuda.enable_math_sdp(True)`
  - Fixes the `CUDA error: invalid configuration argument` caused by FlashAttention failing on short sequence lengths ($T=2$) on Windows CUDA 12.1.
- **Data Loading & High-Performance Disk Caching**:
  - On-demand streaming from zarr chunks via `FrameWindowDataset`, eliminating high RAM overhead while running fast NVMe I/O.
  - Per-movie disk-cached metadata extraction system (`load_cached_video_data` in `experiments/local_training/metadata_cache/`).
  - Speeds up startup dataset metadata loading from 10-15 minutes down to < 0.05 seconds on subsequent runs.
- **Pretrained Weights**: Seeded from `artifacts/pilkwang_support50/weights/unet_transformer/split_0/edge_predictor_best.pth` (SHA256: `12f6881ee3620a831697ca098ff8f48e687a24225f4e048b538deec3562fe771`).
- **Verified Validation**: Completed CUDA training and validation loop, achieving 0.9999 validation accuracy and 0.980 validation score on held-out movies.
- **Automated Kaggle Dataset Deployment** (`src/upload_local_checkpoint_dataset.py`):
  - Checkpoint packaged and published to private Kaggle dataset: `taeyangg4/biohub-local-4070ti-weights`.
  - Available for direct mount into Kaggle inference notebooks (`/kaggle/input/biohub-local-4070ti-weights/best_local_unet_transformer.pth`).



## 16. Local post-processing replay harness (`src/eval_pp_variants_local.py`)

- **What it does**: executes a 12-cell harmonic-fusion-lineage notebook's own config + post-processing + validator-scoring code (code cells 0-2, 5, 8; install/inference/submission skipped) and replays cached ILP prediction graphs under named variants (`{"label": {"NOTEBOOK_GLOBAL": value}}`). Score = size-weighted adjusted edge Jaccard + 0.1 x micro division Jaccard, i.e. the validator's PROXY_SCORE / the official formula.
- **Inputs by default**: the 12 ILP graphs C004's Kaggle run saved under `tmp/c004_log/tracking_repo/predictions` (val8 = C004's validator movies, vis4 = visible test, both with GT in `data/train`), DeepCenter gate from `artifacts/pilkwang_deepcenter` (epoch 2, 8-view TTA as on Kaggle). Frames/heatmaps are cached per movie so all variants see identical DeepCenter scores.
- **Calibration**: Reyhan (C004) and HF/x138 post-processing functions are logically identical (AST-normalized diff = names/prints only). The harness reproduces C004's Kaggle validator table exactly: 0.935924 / 0.951094 / 0.952350.
- **Limits**: readmit and the low-detection gap filler read a dump written during inference (`--lowdet-dir`), so they are idle on C004 graphs; the graphs come from Reyhan inference, not x138's; GT is sparse (~800 edges per movie), so per-movie numbers are noisy — trust effects that agree across val8/vis4 and 44b6/6bba.
- **Runtime**: global Python (torch 2.5.1+cu121, tracksdata 0.1.0rc6), ~1.5 min per variant for all 12 movies on the RTX 4070 Ti SUPER.
- **Usage**: `python src/eval_pp_variants_local.py --notebook <nb> --variants <json> --stems all12 --out reports/pp_replay/<name>.csv [--weak-leaf-prune-from <amanatar nb>]`

- **Options added 2026-09-24/25** (inert unless set): `--round-coords` (integer output coordinates as on Kaggle), `--pred-root` / `--lowdet-dir` (local e2e graphs and low-detection dumps from `src/run_kaggle_predict_local.py`), `--env KEY=VALUE`, `--reinject-ilp-forks` (ILP_FORK_*; closed, section 21), `--stabilize-relink` (STAB_MIN_UM; 4.0 = C017, 0.001 = every pair = C020 / C022), `--ilp-edge-restore` (V1057_MIN_PROB; 0.7 = C021 / C022), `--weak-edge-filter` (WEAK_EDGE_MIN_PROB; amanatar, negative). The candidate notebooks built from these options embed the same code, and each build was replay-checked (notebook == harness on the held-out 12).
- **Validation protocol of the final phase (97 movies)**: held-out 12 (`experiments/candidates/c012_v1284_head/heldout_stems.txt`, includes visible-4), confirm 10 (6bba, unseen by head v1, `confirm_stems.txt`), 75 division-rich movies (`experiments/candidates/c016_division_scorer/stems_b0{0,1,2}.txt`, e2e graphs in `c016_division_scorer/e2e/head_v1_b0*`). All local movies are training movies for the public detectors, so the protocol ranks post-processing only; heads are ranked by the LB (section 7).

Findings on x138 post-processing (2026-09-23, 12 movies): as configured 0.9424; + C004 wide division envelope 0.9354; flow prior off 0.9347; tight 5.2 um 0.9394; weak-leaf prune 0.30/0.40 0.9427.

## 18. Head candidates on the LB (2026-09-23 results) and lessons

| candidate | head | readmit | local e2e all12 | local 6bba subgroup (div tp/fp/fn) | public LB |
|---|---|---|---:|---:|---:|
| C011 twin (thtennant readmit-v1) | none | on | 0.9367 | 0.9311 (1/3/10) | 0.946 |
| C013 | v1 x0.5 | on | 0.9425 | 0.9381 (1/3/10) | 0.948 |
| **C012** | **v1 (30 movies, 85 % 6bba pairs)** | **on** | 0.9470 | **0.9510 (2/2/9)** | **0.952** |
| C014 | v2 (71 movies, balanced) | off | 0.9485 | 0.9431 (1/4/10) | 0.949 |

- The LB ranking (C012 > C014 > C013) matches the local **6bba subgroup** ranking exactly, not the all-12 ranking (where C014 led by 0.0007). 6bba movies carry most of the metric weight (more annotated edges and divisions), so 44b6-side gains (v2's +0.011 adj-edge on 44b6) barely move the LB.
- On 6bba the adjusted edge Jaccard of C012 and C014 is nearly equal (0.9356 vs 0.9364); the gap comes from divisions (2/2/9 vs 1/4/10). Locally, turning readmit off with a head added a false division for both v1 and v2 (3/2 -> 3/3, 3/4 -> 3/5 over 12 movies): with the head the ILP drops more detections, and readmit refills open track starts that safe-division repair would otherwise attach as extra daughters. Hypothesis, not proven (division counts are tiny locally).
- Same head at half strength (C013) keeps about a third of C012's gain: stronger (full) shifts help on the LB.
- Local -> LB transfer for the v1 family: +0.0103 -> +0.006 (C012), +0.0058 -> +0.002 (C013), i.e. roughly 0.3-0.6x.
- Rules going forward: keep x138's recipe (readmit ON) with the head; rank candidates on the 6bba subgroup first and watch division FP; change one thing per candidate.

### Local -> LB calibration (6 anchors with exactly matching local configurations)

Anchors: thtennant readmit-v1 (= C011) 0.946, thtennant gapfill-v1 0.947, thtennant flow2-v1 0.947, C013 0.948, C014 0.949, C012 0.952; local = end-to-end on the 12 held-out movies with the same configuration (`reports/pp_replay/e2e_*_summary.csv`).

| local measure | Pearson with LB | linear fit | max residual |
|---|---:|---|---:|
| **6bba subgroup total (6 movies)** | **+0.994** | **LB = 0.298 x local + 0.668** | **0.0004** |
| visible-4 | +0.954 | LB = 0.299 x local + 0.675 | 0.0011 |
| all 12 (used to pick C014) | +0.836 | LB = 0.398 x local + 0.573 | 0.0021 |
| 6bba adjusted edge only | +0.862 | | 0.0019 |
| 44b6 subgroup | -0.590 | | |

- Use `predicted LB = 0.298 x (local 6bba total) + 0.668` to rank future candidates; a +0.01 local 6bba gain is worth about +0.003 LB.
- Out-of-sample check (2026-09-24 13:35): x138's own head (public LB 0.953, dataset anvithpothula/biohub-v1284-head-s075) run through the C012 pipeline locally gives 6bba total 0.9497 -> predicted 0.9510, residual +0.002 - the largest so far; C015 (0.951) predicted 0.9519, residual -0.001. The formula's practical precision is about +-0.002 LB, so local differences below ~0.007 in the 6bba total are not decidable.
- The fit is post hoc (6 points, same data); it must be re-checked against the next submission before trusting it more.
- The 6bba subset has 6 movies and 11 GT divisions, so one division event moves it by ~0.005-0.008. Extending the local 6bba set with train movies that a given head never saw (head v1 used 30 movies; >100 6bba movies are unseen by it) is the cheapest way to reduce that noise.

Predicted LB 0.952-0.953; it also isolates the readmit question that C014 confounded with head v2. Kernel `taeyangg4/biohub-c015-v1284-head-v1-noreadmit` pushed 2026-09-24 01:30 KST. Run COMPLETE 01:44 KST (head loaded, readmit inactive; T4 vs local refined coords |diff| median 2.4e-4 um; visible-4 0.9229 = local). SUBMITTED 2026-09-23 16:47 UTC, ref 56499136 -> **0.951** (below C012's 0.952): readmit stays ON with the head. The 12-movie 6bba proxy predicted 0.9513 (correct after rounding); the 10-movie confirmation set's +0.0022 was noise.

Overfitting guard for future candidates: rank on the 6bba proxy, require edge-driven gains with majority-of-movies consistency, confirm on unseen movies before spending a submission, one change per candidate. (Superseded 2026-09-25/26: the 6bba proxy over-predicted C017 by ~0.003 and locally consistent post-processing gains lost on the LB - use the section 7 lessons and section 23.)

### Node-count term and minimum track length (2026-09-24 01:55 KST)

- Official adjusted Jaccard = J x (1 - 0.1 x (T_pred - T_true)/T_true), no absolute value: fewer predicted nodes than the organiser's estimate is a bonus. Test zarr metadata carries no `estimated_number_of_nodes`, so per-movie calibration is impossible. On 22 local movies C012 nets +0.0028 from this term (7/22 movies over-predict; 6bba_07e24132 +30 % nodes = -0.027 on that movie).
- Uniform `OUTPUT_MIN_TRACK_LEN` screen on C012 graphs: selection set 7/8 -> +0.0018/+0.0024 (6bba), but the 10 unseen movies give 7 -> 0.0000 (-9 edge TP cancel the node bonus), 8 -> -0.0001 (-20 TP), 5 -> +0.0014 (+21 TP). Direction flips between sets -> no robust uniform setting; lever closed.
- Public LB snapshot (3,863 teams): team rank 111 at 0.952; 0.953 ~ rank 87, 0.955 ~ 60; cutoffs bronze >= 0.947 (386), silver >= 0.949 (193), gold >= 0.963 (18).
- Remaining levers ranked: (1) learned division scorer (local division recall 20 %, term worth up to +0.01-0.015), (2) edge-model retraining as replacement (not blending), (3) head architecture; post-processing knobs are exhausted (gains vanish on unseen movies).

7th anchor (2026-09-24): C015 = C012 + readmit off, local 6bba 0.9508 -> predicted 0.9513 -> LB 0.951 (correct). Lesson: the fitted 12-movie 6bba proxy has now matched 7/7 submissions within rounding; the extra 10-movie 'confirmation' set is not more reliable (it said +0.0022, LB said -0.001). Differences below ~0.003 local (~0.001 LB) are noise on either set; only pursue changes that promise >= 0.01 local.

### Division failure diagnosis (2026-09-24, C012 configuration, `src/diagnose_divisions_local.py`)

22 local movies (12 held-out + 10 unseen 6bba), 24 GT divisions: **tp 6, no_fork 14 (58 %), detection_miss 4** (every miss is an undetected daughter; parents are always matched). Predicted forks: 281, of which only 5 sit on a GT parent that divides, 8 on GT nodes that do not divide, 268 on unannotated cells (invisible to the metric locally, but the 5:8 ratio on annotated parents suggests most forks are wrong). Conclusion: division recall is mostly a *linking* failure - daughters exist as nodes but the graph has no fork at the parent. `src/diagnose_divisions_detail.py` breaks the 14 no_fork cases down (which daughter is linked elsewhere, distances, safe-division geometry, candidate edge probabilities).

## 20. C016 division scorer (started 2026-09-24 02:40 KST, user-approved)

Goal: replace x138's rule-based safe-division acceptance (parent <= 9 um, sister <= 14 um, symmetry tau 0.6, DeepCenter veto) with a learned scorer over candidate triples (P at t, its linked daughter D1 and a second daughter D2 at t+1, parent <= 14 um, sister <= 16 um, no symmetry rule, D2 may be re-parented from a weak competing edge). Diagnosis: 14/24 local GT divisions are 'no_fork' with exactly this asymmetric geometry (one daughter 1-2 um from P, the other 7-14 um). Expected: local division Jaccard 0.19 -> ~0.4, LB +0.005-0.007 if half of them are recovered without extra FPs on annotated parents.

Data: GT divisions are sparse - 151 in 199 train movies (12 held-out: 15, confirm-10: 9, remaining 177 movies: 127, 71 movies with >= 1). Local C012-config inference (head v1, candidate mode) is being run on the remaining movies in division-rich order (`experiments/candidates/c016_division_scorer/batch_*.txt`, output `e2e/head_v1_bNN/`). Features are restricted to what post-processing can see on Kaggle (geometry, admitted edge probabilities from the low-detection dump, detection scores, DeepCenter heatmap); labels mirror the official `score_divisions()` (vendored metric): 1 = the fork would be paired with a GT division (P or its predecessor matches the GT divider or its parent, the two GT daughter lineages - daughter or grand-daughter - are matched in the two distinct predicted branches), 0 = a fork the metric counts as FP (considered for a GT division but not paired; P matched to a GT node with >= 1 child; or the two branches carry matched evidence from two different GT tracks - even at an unannotated P), -1 = not counted (P unmatched or matched to a GT track end) and excluded from training. Earlier drafts labelled every annotated parent as 0/1; that over-counted track ends as negatives and missed cross-track FPs at unannotated parents (fixed 10:15 KST, all candidate CSVs regenerated). Held-out 12 stay the final end-to-end test; embryo-grouped CV for the scorer.

### Progress 2026-09-24 (10:20 KST)
- Local C012-config inference on the 75 division-rich movies: b00 done (25 movies, ~2 min/movie), b01/b02 running sequentially (`e2e/head_v1_b01`, `_b02`; ETA ~11:45). Candidate generation (`src/division_candidates_local.py`, module-based so training features == notebook features) chained automatically after each batch; held-out-12 and confirm-10 candidates are being regenerated with the v2 features (`candidates_heldout12.csv`, `candidates_confirm10.csv`).
- 22-movie pilot (v1 features, `candidates_eval22.csv`): 2,745,434 candidates, 63,255 labelled (parent matched to an annotated GT node), only **19 positives** (24 GT divisions in these movies -> 79 % are candidate-recoverable). Positives are geometrically distinct (asym median 0.59 vs 1.52 for negatives, cos(D1,D2) -0.85 vs -0.01, `prob_q_d2` 0.0 vs 0.88) but the metric needs ~0.01 % FP rate: a scorer trained on 19 positives reached only 7 % precision at 32 % recall (movie-grouped OOF), so the extra positives from the 75 movies are essential. No cheap pre-filter removes negatives without losing positives (D2 free or `prob_q_d2` < 0.95 keeps 85 % of candidates and 19/19 positives) and none is needed for runtime: the C012 kernel takes 16 min for the visible 4 movies (12 h limit), the stage is ~1-2 min on the densest movie.
- Key finding: x138's rule only considers **orphan** D2 (`candidate_ids = ... not in incoming`), while most positives have D2 already linked to some Q by post-processing (motion relink / gap filling, `edge_prob` None). Feature set v2 (40 features, `src/division_scorer_stage.py`): origin of the Q->D2 edge (`q_d2_relinked`, `q_d2_gap`), Q's in-degree / children / track length / detection score / synthetic flag (`readmitted`, `gap_synthetic`, `gapfill_peak`), detection peak at D2's position one frame earlier (`det_t_at_d2`, `_dist`: a newborn daughter has none, a continuing cell has one), track lengths, synthetic flags of P/D1/D2.
- Acceptance safety: `add_scored_divisions` never re-parents a D2 whose ILP edge probability is >= `BIOHUB_DIVISION_REPARENT_MAX_PROB` (0.95), on top of the score thresholds and x138's global cap (0.375 % of nodes).
- Baseline under the official metric (`reports/pp_replay/e2e_head_v1_all12.csv`, C012 config): held-out 12 division TP 3 / FP 2 / FN 12 (micro J 0.176; 6bba subgroup TP 2 / FP 2 / FN 9, J 0.154). Marginal value: one recovered division = +1/17 J (+0.0059 score), one extra false fork = -3/(17*18) J (-0.0010 score) -> break-even precision J/(1+J) ~ 0.15. Recovering 3 / 5 / 7 misses with no new FP = +0.018 / +0.029 / +0.041 local (6bba +0.023 / +0.039 / +0.054), i.e. ~+0.005 to +0.012 LB through the 0.298 slope.
- Go/no-go before any upload: movie-grouped OOF precision >= 0.35 on labelled candidates (2x the break-even, covering hidden-test shift) at a recall that recovers >= 3 held-out-12 divisions, then end-to-end 6bba proxy on the held-out 12 (`src/verify_c016.py --replay`) must beat C012 by >= 0.005 local on the majority of 6bba movies with divisions. Otherwise C016 is not submitted and C012 (0.952) stays.

### Result 12:10 KST: C016 is a NO-GO (scorer cannot reach the precision the metric needs)
- v3 features, MLP(32), strict positives only, trained on b00 + b01 + confirm-10 (60 movies, 68 strict positive candidates on 37 parents vs 147,752 labelled negatives), movie-grouped 5-fold OOF, evaluated per parent (best candidate per P, as the stage accepts): threshold 0.9 -> 17 TP / 142 FP (10.7 %, recall 0.25); held-out 12 (never trained on): 2 TP / 30 FP (6 %), all FPs 'evaluable' (annotated parents that do not divide). Pre-registered go criterion was >= 35 % at >= 3 held-out TPs. v2 features were no better (MLP all kinds: OOF 23 % at 0.9, held-out 10 %; logistic 12 % / 11 %).
- Full grid with b02 (85 training movies, all label kinds): MLP(32) OOF per-parent at 0.9 = 66 TP / 223 FP (22.8 %, recall 0.30), held-out 12 = 6 TP / 65 FP (8.5 %); logistic 19.8 % OOF / 12.5 % held-out (3 TP / 21 FP). Same verdict.
- Full grid, strict positives only (85 movies, 85 strict positive candidates): MLP(32) OOF per-parent at 0.9 = 31 TP / 101 FP (23.5 %, recall 0.37) - the best OOF of the grid - but held-out 12 = 0 TP / 3 FP at 0.9, 1 TP / 5 FP at 0.8. Grid complete 12:10 KST; verdict unchanged.
- Top-k analysis of the same scores: OOF top-20 parents over 60 movies = 9 TP / 11 FP (45 %, 0.33 acceptances per movie) - an ultra-conservative threshold (>= 0.9999) would be worth roughly +0.0007 local per movie (~+0.0002 LB), inside the noise and exposed to the hidden-test degradation; held-out top-5 = 1 TP / 4 FP. Not worth a submission.
- Why: the base rate. ~2.5 recoverable strict divisions per movie against ~5,000 labelled candidate parents; the metric needs ~0.02 % false-fork rate for 35 % precision. The best single signals (asym AUC 0.86, no detection at D2's position one frame earlier 0.83, cos 0.74, sister growth 0.71, parent brightness 0.64) do not combine to that specificity, and the one signal that would (the transformer's own P->D2 probability) does not exist because the ILP candidate radius never proposes the far daughter. Matches the forum: hikaggler's trained division head (+0.12 local divJ) moved the LB +-0.001; OpPrime / kevin park found no dependable division gain on this stack.
- Everything stays reusable (stage module, labeller mirroring the official metric, per-parent evaluation, candidate CSVs for 97 movies with 48 features); nothing was uploaded or pushed. C012 (0.952) remains the best entry.

### Feature set v3 and label kinds (11:30 KST)
- Preliminary scorer on the pre-rule v2 data (b00 + confirm-10 = 132 positives / 105K labelled negatives, logistic regression, movie-grouped CV): OOF average precision 0.048; at threshold 0.9 22 TP / 157 FP (12 %), held-out 12: 5 TP / 39 FP - below the 15 % break-even. The weak part is parent-level evidence ("does P divide at all"): with the metric-faithful labels the positives' geometry is much less clean than in the strict pilot (asym median 1.11 vs 1.47 for negatives, cos(D1,D2) -0.50 vs -0.04).
- v3 features (48, `src/division_scorer_stage.py`): raw-frame intensity normalised per frame ((v - median) / (p99.5 - median) of a strided subsample; frames are only (64, 256, 256) uint16, 0.02 s to read): int_p_peak / int_p_mean (dividing parents are bright and condensed - Canh measured AUC 0.73), int_p_prev_peak, int_d1_peak, int_d2_peak / int_d2_mean, int_mid_mean (between the daughters), plus sister_t2 (distance between the daughters' children at t+2). `make_intensity_fn(dataset, read_test_frame, frame_cache)` lives in the stage module so the notebook computes it identically; parents are visited in frame order for the bounded frame cache.
- Label kinds recorded per candidate (`label_kind`): positives strict / early / grand, negatives considered / evaluable / cross; `division_scorer_train.py --positive-kinds strict` trains on the clean positives only (the others are neither clean positives nor negatives and are dropped from training; evaluation keeps every label).
- Per-parent evaluation added to the trainer (best candidate per P, as the stage accepts them) - the all-candidate threshold table overstates FPs.
- All candidate sets regenerated with v3 + kinds from 11:30 (b00, b01, held-out 12, confirm 10 in parallel; b02 automatically after its inference). The machine is shared with another project's workers (`kaggriculture-strategy-meta validation_v3.py`, 8+ processes), so everything runs ~2x slower than the 10:00 timings.
- v3 data check (b01, 25 movies, 11:46): 42,926 labelled candidates, 60 positives = 25 strict + 34 early + 1 grand; negatives 42,202 evaluable + 458 considered + 206 cross. Feature AUC vs negatives for STRICT positives: asym 0.86, det_t_at_d2 0.83, cos(D1,D2) 0.74, sister_t2 0.71, int_p_peak 0.64, int_p_prev_peak 0.64, int_d1_peak 0.61, int_mid_mean 0.59 (inverted). For ALL positives the geometry collapses (asym 0.68, det 0.66) because 'early' triples (fork one frame before the division through the far daughter's wrong parent) are geometrically ordinary - so the scorer is trained on strict positives only (config C); early/grand forks still count as TP at evaluation.

### Design change 10:35 KST: C016 is additive (x138 rule first, scorer second)
The placeholder replay (random scorer replacing the rule) lost all 3 divisions x138's rule finds on the held-out 12 (44b6_341df25f, 6bba_062c8d37, 6bba_09961292 -> TP 0). Replacing the rule therefore risks C012's own TPs; the scorer now runs AFTER the unchanged rule and only sees parents the rule left single-child. Candidate generation captures the post-rule graph (`src/division_candidates_local.py`); candidate CSVs produced before 10:35 (pre-rule graph, a superset) are used only for preliminary model selection and are regenerated for the final training set.

### Kaggle discussion intelligence (read 2026-09-24 10:20-10:35 KST, in-app browser)
- **All local movies are in-sample for the public detectors.** `artifacts/pilkwang_temporal_seed314159/.../split_manifest.json` (method `unet_transformer_alltrain_seed314159_v1`) lists 199 train entries that include the 4 visible-test movies and every held-out / confirm / batch movie; DeepCenter trained on 71 (6 of the held-out 12, 0 of the confirm 10, 21 of the 75 scorer-training movies). Eric (discussion 742064) reports the same and a concrete +0.0078 local -> -0.002 LB example; hikaggler (741749): a trained division head lifted local division J 0.07 -> 0.19 but moved the LB +-0.001; Pavel (739352): an edge-weight model +0.02-0.04 on every fold, +0.000 LB. This is the likely reason for the damped local -> LB slope (0.298) and means every local division-scorer number is an upper bound; the hidden test sees detections the UNet never memorised (more missed / displaced daughters).
- Division geometry base rates from the labels (Le Quang Canh, 740573): division step median 4.6 um (IQR 3.3-6.2, p90 7.1, max 12.3); between *predicted* nodes the same events read median 8.5, max 13.7 um (detector localisation error at division time); sister separation median 8.85 (max 14.65); continuation step median 1.72, p99 6.9. Steps > 5 um: 24 continuations per division. Our candidate gate (parent <= 14, sister <= 16) covers all of it; geometry alone cannot separate (base rate), pair properties (sister, symmetry, direction) can. Appearance: parent peak intensity at the split frame AUC 0.73 (possible v3 feature).
- Metric facts confirmed by others: over-detection almost free, under-prediction bonus unbounded (organisers aware, expected behaviour); metric patched 17 July (divisions need directed local topology, multi-frame edges dropped); duplicate detections cost ~9 % and buy nothing; Lime1123: divisions are sensitive to localisation within ~3 um although matching uses 7 um.
- Top players: Tang (3rd) - work order detection -> linking -> division, build a reliable CV and trust it, external/synthetic data for division robustness; Ogurtsov (5th) - the remaining gap is reachable from the published UNet "to some extent but pretty risky"; TWEAK (25th) - a graph-refinement "plugin" adds 0.03-0.05 to any public notebook (no details); Antonoof (25th): deleting 45 % of nodes/edges did not change his 0.954 (later retracted as probably his error).
- Shake-up: public LB = 29 % of the test; across 13 past microscopy competitions the medal zone kept 36-97 %; movie-to-movie spread on leave-one-embryo-out is +-0.14. Team-merge/entry deadline passed 2026-09-23; submissions count until 2026-09-29 (Satwik, 742702). Our rank ~111 (silver cutoff ~0.949) is exposed to noise either way; keep C012 as one of the two final picks.

## 21. Ideas backlog after C016 (2026-09-24 12:10 KST) - every item below was analysed and closed; later work is in sections 22-24

### Node budget vs the organiser's estimate (analysis only, `estimated_number_of_nodes` from the train geff metadata, C012-config ILP node counts on 97 movies)
- T_pred / T_true: median 0.972 overall; 6bba (n=70) median 0.978, p10 0.92, p90 1.14, 10 movies over-predict > 10 %, 4 over 20 % (6bba_207c6aaf +48 %, 6bba_ab78413d +43 %, 6bba_07e24132 +29 %, 6bba_fc5f39dc +27 %: node-term cost 0.025-0.044 on those movies); 44b6 (n=27) median 0.79 (the organiser's estimate is up to 2.6x our count - a free under-prediction bonus, e.g. 44b6_0b24845f adj J 1.04).
- Predictability of T_true from our own count: 6bba log-log fit R2 0.984, residual ~10 %; 44b6 residual ~39 % (unusable). The 6bba over-prediction outliers are NOT explained by T_pred (they are the residuals), so a per-movie node budget would need a better regressor (candidates: low-detection score histogram, synthetic-node counts, intensity statistics).
- **Analysis 1 result (12:15 KST, `src/node_budget_ttrue_fit.py`, 97 movies, leave-one-out)**: 6bba T_true from T_pred alone has LOO residual 10.4 % and NO low-detection peak-count feature (thresholds 0.2-0.9) improves it; the model is essentially T_true ~ T_pred, so the +27..+48 % over-predicted movies are pure residuals and cannot be flagged from our own counts (0 of 70 flagged). 44b6: 42 % -> 17 % with two peak counts, still unusable (flags are mostly false alarms). => per-movie adaptive trimming is CLOSED; only a global rule that removes a near-zero-match node group could work (Analysis 2, `src/node_budget_analysis.py`, running on the 22 evaluation movies).
- Trimming economics: only nodes that are (almost) never GT-matched can be removed. Expected loss of removing a random node = 2 GT edges x P(annotated) ~ 2/T_pred, gain of the node term ~ 0.1 J / T_true ~ 0.1/T_pred -> random trimming loses 20x. A trimming set needs a match rate < 0.5 % (6bba average 8.4 %). Unknown whether such a group exists (candidates: lowest detection-score tracks, readmitted nodes, gap-fill synthetic nodes) - measurable on the 22 eval movies with the harness before any build.
- **Analysis 2 result (12:30 KST, `src/node_budget_analysis.py`, 22 evaluation movies, final post-processed C012 graphs, 7 um bipartite matching)**: 6bba (16 movies, 358,180 nodes) mean match rate 4.28 % -> a group pays for removal only below ~0.20 %. Lowest groups: readmitted nodes 0.55 % (1.2 % of nodes), post-processing-only edges 0.90 %, max incident edge prob < 0.5 0.79 %, tracks of length 5-9 0.66 % (4.6 %), DeepCenter < 0.05 2.5 %, detection peak < 0.2 3.7 %, dim nodes (intensity < 0.15) 3.1 %. **No group is below the break-even**; the best one (readmitted, 2.8x above) is exactly what C015 tested on the LB (readmit off 0.951 < C012 0.952). 44b6 (6 movies, 157,905 nodes) mean match rate 1.01 % -> break-even 0.047 %: dim nodes 0.06 % (3 %), DeepCenter < 0.05 0.14 %, short tracks 0.10 % - none below, and 44b6 already sits far below T_true (bonus). Short tracks <= 4 and isolated nodes do not exist after x138's own filters. Official re-scoring of the least-matched groups' removal: see `reports/node_budget/rules_scored.txt`.
- Official re-scoring of removal rules on the 22 movies (`reports/node_budget/rules_scored.txt`, 6bba total / all-22 / 44b6): DeepCenter < 0.05 -0.0405 / -0.0368 / -0.0024 (single movies down to -0.40: low-DeepCenter nodes sit inside real tracks); dim nodes (intensity < 0.15) -0.0328 / -0.0298 / -0.0016; readmitted nodes +0.0010 / +0.0009 / -0.0006 (11/5 movies better on 6bba, and +0.0007 of it is one fewer false fork); nodes whose only edges have prob < 0.5 +0.0012 / +0.0007 / -0.0040; tracks <= 4 nodes: none exist. The analytic break-even (2 TP edges per matched node) was too pessimistic - the two least-matched groups come out slightly positive - but the gains are +0.001 local (~+0.0003 LB), inside the noise and below the >= 0.01 local bar set after C014/C015; C015 (readmit off at pipeline level) already lost 0.001 on the LB.
- **Verdict: node-budget idea CLOSED.** No over-prediction detector (Analysis 1) and no removable node group with a gain above noise (Analysis 2); the metric's node term cannot be harvested without paying more in edges. Tools kept: `src/node_budget_ttrue_fit.py`, `src/node_budget_analysis.py` (per-node attributes + match flags cached in `reports/node_budget/cache/*.pkl`).
- Expected value if everything works: ~+0.0026 local on 6bba (~+0.001 LB), i.e. noise-level on average but it removes a -0.03/-0.04 tail on heavily over-predicted movies. Not recommended without both analyses succeeding.

### Transformer P->D2 check on the held-out divisions (13:05 KST, `src/division_edge_prob_check.py`, dense dump `e2e/head_v1_dense`, BIOHUB_CACHE_EDGE_THRESHOLD=0.02)
- The edge transformer scores EVERY (t, t+1) pair (no radius) with a softmax over each child's parent candidates; the ILP admits pairs > 0.48 and forks only when the second child's probability exceeds the ILP division weight (edge weight -1.0 x prob, division weight 1.2, appearance 0, disappearance 2).
- 9 strict GT division events (P = divider, D1/D2 = daughters) on 6 held-out movies: p(P->D2) = 0.799, 0.713 (both already ILP-admitted; the fork is blocked ONLY by the 1.2 division penalty, since p2 < 1.2), 0.380 (rank 1, competitor 0.138; needs candidate threshold < 0.38 and division weight < 0.38), 0.170 / 0.111 / 0.024 (rank 2 behind a nearer competitor at 0.50 / 0.78 / 0.90), and 0.000 x 3 (far daughters 10-13 um with a 1-2 um competitor at ~0.9). Median p(P->D2) 0.11, competitor median 0.50.
- Reading: 2 of 9 (maybe 3) held-out misses are an ILP-parameter artefact, 6 of 9 are genuine model limits (only retraining could change them). A local test of `BIOHUB_ILP_DIVISION_WEIGHT` 1.2 -> ~0.7 (forks only when the transformer gives the second child > 0.7) is cheap (re-inference of the 12 held-out movies + harness) and has never been on the LB; FP risk is bounded by the transformer's own confidence. Not started (user approved the 1-hour analysis only).

- **ILP division weight test (14:30 KST, `experiments/candidates/c017_ilp_division/`, held-out 12, head v1)**: with `BIOHUB_ILP_DIVISION_WEIGHT` 0.7 the ILP makes **578 forks** (second-child probability 0.70-0.95, median 0.77) and with 0.5 **1,838 forks**, against 15 GT divisions - the transformer's second-child confidence is not division evidence (precision <= 2.6 % even at 0.7; break-even ~15 %). x138's Hungarian relink discards them all, so the final graphs barely change (per-movie adj |diff| <= 0.0015), yet the 6bba total still drops 0.9510 -> 0.9428 because the slightly different ILP node/edge set flips one rule-based division in 6bba_09961292 (2/2/9 -> 1/2/10; one division event = ~0.008 on the 6bba total). Both weights give identical final graphs on 9 of 12 movies. Fork re-injection scoring (`--reinject-ilp-forks`, `reports/pp_replay/ilp_forks_head_v1_ilpdiv0.7_all12.csv`): re-adding the ILP forks before the rule stage gives 6bba divisions 3/11/8 at p >= 0.7 without re-parenting (one more TP, nine more FP; 6bba total 0.9495), 3/14/8 with re-parenting (0.9478), 3/26/8 at p >= 0.5 (0.9431), 1/5/10 at p >= 0.85 (0.9417) - every variant below the C012 baseline 0.9510. => the "ILP-parameter artefact" reading of the P->D2 check was too optimistic: the two true divisions with p >= 0.7 sit among ~576 false forks at the same confidence. Lever closed.

### x138 author's own V1284 head is now public (found by the user 2026-09-24 12:50 KST)
- Dataset `anvithpothula/biohub-v1284-head-s075` (CC0, created 2026-09-20, one file `v1284_head.pt` 33,913 bytes, SHA256 625a0d9340f48193f2ec294fc2d81c5bb3c03087eab78ef0ae998a9c4c7da00c, description "produced 0.953 score in anvithpothula/biohub-x138"). Local copy: `artifacts/anvithpothula_v1284_head_s075/v1284_head.pt`. Same format and architecture as our heads ({state_dict Linear(224,32)/SiLU/Linear(32,3), mean, scale}); loads with weights_only=True.
- x138's own comment (cell 4): trained on x107's 20-movie TRAIN capture (4,136 detection<->GT pairs); held-out by movie it moves centres closer to truth (ridge -10.8 %, MLP -10.4 % pooled, 16 of 20 movies improve; a 4-movie version was +14.9 % worse). Our head v1 (187 movies) scored 0.952 vs their 0.953 - within noise.
- **Held-out-12 capture pairs (13:15 KST, `pairs_heldout/`, 7,753 pairs; neither head trained on these movies)**: pooled centre error base 1.596 um -> ours v1 1.255 (-21.4 %), x138 1.414 (-11.4 %), average 1.294. But the heads are embryo-specific: **44b6** (1,524 pairs) x138 -20.2 % vs v1 -12.6 % / v2 -14.6 % / v3 -12.2 % (x138 wins 5 of 6 movies); **6bba** (6,229 pairs) v3 -26.1 % / v1 -23.5 % vs x138 -9.2 % (ours win 6 of 6). Confirm-10 (all 6bba): v1 -22.7 % vs x138 -9.5 %. => a prefix-routed head (44b6 -> x138 s075, 6bba -> ours) takes the better head on every movie; both components are LB-proven (0.953 / 0.952). The end-to-end check needs no new inference: combine the per-movie rows of the x138-head run (queued) and the v1 run by prefix.
- **Pair-level error does NOT predict end-to-end score (13:20 KST)**: head v3 has the best 6bba pair error (-26.1 %) but the worst 6bba e2e (6bba total 0.9369 vs v1 0.9510; adj 0.9298 vs 0.9356; div 1/3/10 vs 2/2/9; `reports/pp_replay/e2e_head_v3_all12.csv`). Per-prefix e2e totals: 44b6 v1 0.9298 / v2 0.9511 / v3 0.9250 / v1_a05 0.9499; 6bba v1 0.9510 / v2 0.9389 / v3 0.9369 / v1_a05 0.9381. Single movies swing by up to 0.075 between heads (44b6_0113de3b: v1 0.885, v2 0.868, v3 0.960, a05 0.922) because sub-micron coordinate changes flip geometry-gated relinks and divisions - the same mechanism behind the C012..C015 LB spread. Consequence: any head-routing decision must rest on the official e2e per prefix (x138-head run queued), not on pair errors, and per-movie evidence from 6 movies per prefix is fragile; treat routing as a hedge pick, not an improvement.
- **x138 head end-to-end (13:35 KST, `reports/pp_replay/e2e_head_x138_s075_all12.csv`, C012 pipeline with their head)**: 6bba total 0.9497 (pred LB 0.9510) vs ours 0.9510 (pred LB 0.9514); 44b6 0.9235 vs 0.9298; all-12 0.9447 vs 0.9470; divisions 3/2/12 both. Per movie |diff| up to 0.03 in both directions (44b6_2a2eff9f -0.031, 44b6_267148e4 +0.009). The proxy puts the two heads within 0.0004 LB of each other, i.e. the public 0.953 vs 0.952 is rounding/noise, and the pair-level 44b6 advantage of their head does not survive the post-processing. Routing by prefix is therefore NOT supported (44b6<-x138 + 6bba<-v1: all-12 0.9457 < v1 alone 0.9470); the v2-on-44b6 combination (all-12 0.9528) rests on one extra 44b6 division out of 4 and is the C014 head that lost on the LB. Head question closed: keep head v1; x138's public head is a legitimate hedge pick (known 0.953), not an improvement. Calibration data point added: pred 0.9510 for a head that scored 0.953 (residual +0.002, the largest so far).

Mounting their dataset at `/kaggle/input/biohub-v1284-head-s075/v1284_head.pt` makes the pulled x138 notebook runnable as-is (= 0.953).

## 22. Public sweep, jump-stabilized relink and the head lane (2026-09-24 .. 2026-09-26)

### 22.1 Sweep and jump-stabilized relink (2026-09-24 afternoon)

- Sweep: radar refreshed (269 notebooks), 55 non-trivial notebooks pulled and audited by four parallel audits (`reports/public_ideas_audit_20260924_{A_learned,B_association,C_fusion_budget,D_alternative}.md`), ~80 technical forum threads read (`reports/untried_ideas_discussions_20260924.md`). Consolidated ranked list: `reports/untried_ideas_20260924.md`. Tried list used as the baseline: `reports/tried_methods_20260924.md`.
- Key forum facts: hidden test = different embryos from 44b6/6bba (host, 716793; same instrument and protocol, 724386); local validation on train movies is in-sample for every public checkpoint and corrective stages look useless there (Mark_RowSet 730160, matches our C015); removing forks costs 0.035 on the LB (alfonso V1329).
- **Frozen frames and whole-field jumps** (forum 724283 / 729082, measured with `src/frame_motion_audit.py`, `reports/frame_motion_audit.csv`): 947 byte-identical consecutive pairs (4.8 %, 114 of 128 6bba movies, none in 44b6), double steps after them (GT median 3.28 um), 1,718 coherent jumps >= 3 um (11.8 %) in 137 movies. C012 misses 3.1 % of GT edges on normal pairs, 7.9 % after frozen pairs, 19.9 % on 5-8 um jumps and 47.5 % above 8 um, while the raw ILP misses only 11.1 % / 27.5 % on those jumps: x138's distance-gated Hungarian relink breaks links the transformer had right.
- **Jump-stabilized relink** (`src/eval_pp_variants_local.py --stabilize-relink`, `STAB_MIN_UM`): per pair, global shift = median displacement of the ILP's own links (p >= 0.5, >= 8 links); if |shift| >= STAB_MIN_UM the relink runs on positions shifted by the cumulative jump sum (geometry only; distances recomputed on the original coordinates). Official metric, head v1: held-out 12 total 0.9470 -> 0.9586 (+0.0117; 8 better / 3 worse, worst -0.0023; divisions unchanged), held-out 6bba 0.9510 -> 0.9613 (+0.0103, predicted LB 0.9514 -> 0.9545), 44b6 +0.0170, confirm-10 0.9404 -> 0.9480 (+0.0077, 6 / 1). Thresholds 2.0 / 2.5 / 3.0 / 4.0 um all within +-0.0005 on the 6bba total. First post-C012 idea that clears the >= 0.01 local bar; C017 (= C012 + this stage) proposed.
- 75-movie robustness (division-rich train batches b00-b02, `reports/pp_replay/stabilize_head_v1_b0{0,1,2}.csv`, stab 3.0 vs as is): all 75 total 0.9316 -> 0.9389 (+0.0073; 44 better / 10 worse / 21 same), 6bba 54 movies +0.0080 (40 / 8 / 6), 44b6 21 movies +0.0011 (4 / 2 / 15), 63 movies never seen by head v1 +0.0068; division FP +2 (37 -> 39). Worst movie 6bba_f8ffd5e7 -0.0336 (next worst -0.0024): a movie with continuous 3-6 um drift in almost every pair, where the threshold flags alternate pairs; x138's seed pass predicts each pair from the previous pair's flow field, so alternating stabilized / unstabilized pairs make that prior inconsistent. Testing 'stabilize every pair' (STAB_MIN_UM ~ 0, consistent drift removal) on all 97 movies before choosing C017's setting.
- 'Stabilize every pair' (STAB_MIN_UM 0.001, `reports/pp_replay/stabilize_all_*.csv`, all 97 movies): mean slightly higher (all 97 +0.0088 vs +0.0077 for 3.0; held-out 6bba +0.0118 vs +0.0103, pred LB 0.9549 vs 0.9545) but more and larger losers (24 vs 14 movies worse; -0.0116, -0.0084 besides f8ffd5e7) and f8ffd5e7 still -0.0287, so the alternation hypothesis does not explain that movie (likely non-rigid drift that x138's local flow field handled better than a global shift). Conservative flagged-pairs-only stabilization preferred; threshold 4.0 being checked on the 75 batch movies.
- Threshold choice on all 97 movies (`reports/pp_replay/stabilize_4_b0*.csv`): 3.0 -> +0.0077 (58 better / 14 worse, worst -0.0336); **4.0 -> +0.0071 (52 / 9, worst -0.0216)**; every pair -> +0.0088 (70 / 24, worst -0.0287). Held-out 6bba: 3.0 +0.0103, 4.0 +0.0098 (pred LB 0.9543), every pair +0.0118; confirm-10: 4.0 +0.0066 with 0 losers. Chosen **4.0** (the mean differences are within noise; 4.0 touches the fewest movies, has the smallest worst case, and at 4 um flags essentially no pair whose GT moves < 2 um). C017 rebuilt with STAB_MIN_UM 4.0 (notebook sha256 9b2ada59...), `verify_c017.py --replay` ALL PASS (held-out 12: 0.9470 -> 0.9583; 6bba 0.9510 -> 0.9608). Pushed as private kernel `taeyangg4/biohub-c017-jump-stabilized-relink` v1 on 2026-09-24 16:37 KST (user: "제출해보도록하자"); Kernel COMPLETE in 15 min; T4 visible-4 official 0.93852 = local prediction 0.9385 (fidelity confirmed); submitted as **56515876** (2026-09-24 07:55 UTC). Expected public LB ~0.955 (0.953-0.957).

### 22.3 LB results of the stabilization family (read 2026-09-25)

- C017 (jump-stabilized relink, 4 um) **0.952** = C012; C018 (+ frozen-frame prior) 0.952; **C020 (every pair stabilized) 0.953; C021 (C017 + V1057 restore 0.7) 0.953; C022 (C020 + restore) 0.953**. Best public score now 0.953 (ties x138's public notebook).
- The 6bba proxy predicted C017 at 0.9543 (anchored on C012's actual: ~0.955); actual 0.952 -> residual -0.002/-0.003, the largest miss of the calibration. Likely reason: the held-out 6bba proxy gain was driven by a few jump-heavy movies (07e24132 +0.025, 05db0fb1 +0.024, 09961292 +0.013), while the public 29 % of the hidden test (other embryos) apparently contains few such acquisition jumps. The small extra gains of C020/C021/C022 (local +0.0017..0.0029 over C017 on 97 movies, mostly outside the held-out 6bba set) did cross one rounding step.
- Implication: the 6bba proxy is not reliable for acquisition-artefact fixes; the 97-movie aggregate ranked C022 > C020 ~ C021 > C018 > C017, consistent with the LB ordering (0.953 x3 > 0.952 x2). Stabilization never hurt the public LB, so a stabilized variant is a sound final pick (private LB = other 71 % of the test, jump prevalence unknown).

**What is established**
- Our post-processing on top of x138 (every-pair jump-stabilized relink + ILP-edge restore) is worth +0.001 on the public LB and +0.007 on 97 local movies; it never hurt on the LB. It is the common core of every finalist.
- The coordinate head is the only other lever with an LB-measured effect: x138's public head +0.001 over ours (two independent observations); the 2-head mean equals x138 alone. Local e2e ranks heads the opposite way (embryo-level leakage: our heads were trained on the same two embryos), so heads are ranked by the LB only.
- Locally selected settings (C025) lost 0.001 on the LB despite +0.0018 on 97 movies: even a locally consistent post-processing gain of ~0.002 is not bankable. Do not add more settings.
- Every other lane is closed with measurements (sections 20-24): division scorer, node budget, ILP forks/costs, relink gates and priors, gap closers, TTA pooling, detection/edge fusion weights, head v4 alone, weak-edge filter, leaf/segment pruning, external-notebook layers, AdaBN test-time adaptation, appearance-based division classifier. The public forum (2026-09-26 refresh) reports the same: single-knob changes on this stack lose on the LB; gains above 0.955 came from retraining, which does not fit the remaining time.

**Final decision**
1. All probes are scored and no further candidates are planned: validated ideas, not slots, are the constraint, and more probes would only select on public noise.
2. Final picks (select in Kaggle before 2026-09-29 23:59 UTC; Kaggle keeps the better private score): **C024 `56541277` + C023 `56538803`** - both 0.954, different head composition (2-head mean vs x138 alone), no settings, shared validated core (every-pair stabilization + ILP-edge restore).
3. Overfitting guard: never choose between candidates that differ by 0.001 on the public LB alone; prefer components with both local and LB support.

## 24. Last-days moonshots (2026-09-26 16:40 KST; user wants +0.01, accepts low odds)

Both approved by the user. Neither has local validation that means anything (the local movies are the training embryos), so they are LB probes with a mechanism.

**(a) AdaBN test-time adaptation** - `src/build_adabn_repo.py` (env `BIOHUB_ADABN=1`, `_MAX_WINDOWS`, `_SECONDARY`): before each movie, the BatchNorm3d running statistics of the primary and secondary UNets are recomputed from that movie's frames (cumulative average over all windows; weights untouched; transformer LayerNorm/Dropout untouched; original statistics restored after the movie). Rationale: the hidden embryos differ from the two training embryos; BN statistics carry intensity/density domain shift. Local run on the held-out 12 with the x138 head (`experiments/candidates/c030_adabn/e2e/adabn_s075`) = sanity check only (in-sample); candidate C030 = C023 + AdaBN.
- **(a) local sanity result 16:42 KST** (`reports/pp_replay/replay_c023_adabn_all12.csv`, both UNets adapted on every window; BN running-mean shift 0.09-0.19): all-12 0.9547 -> 0.9543 (-0.0004); **6bba +0.0047 (nodes -6 %), 44b6 -0.0193 (nodes +11 %, 1 better / 5 worse)**; divisions 3/2/12 -> 3/1/12; visible-4 0.9334 -> 0.9267. AdaBN changes the detection count strongly and embryo-dependently even on the training embryos; the secondary UNet carries 80 % of the detection blend, so a primary-only variant (`BIOHUB_ADABN_SECONDARY=0`) is being run before deciding on the probe. C030 (both UNets) is built (sha 5273325f) but not pushed.
- **(a) primary-only variant (17:00 KST, `replay_c023_adabn_primary_all12.csv`)**: all-12 -0.0009; 6bba +0.0062 (nodes -4 %); **44b6 -0.0231 (nodes +11 %, one division lost)**. The instability is not the secondary model: adapting BN statistics on a 44b6 movie makes the primary UNet over-detect by ~11 %. Embryo-dependent swings of +-0.02 on the training embryos mean the hidden-embryo effect is unpredictable and the downside is larger than any bankable gain. **AdaBN CLOSED per the pre-stated rule (push only if stable on both embryos); C030 not pushed.** Tools kept: `src/build_adabn_repo.py`, `src/build_c030_candidate.py` (notebook sha 5273325f) for the record.

**(b) Appearance-based division classifier** - `src/division_crops_extract.py` (GT parents with two children = positives, one-child GT nodes away from divisions = negatives; 3 frames x (8, 32, 32) full-res crops, movie quantile normalisation) + `src/division_cnn_train.py` (small 3D CNN, movie-grouped 5-fold CV, precision/recall per threshold). Go/no-go: OOF precision at recall 0.5 >= 0.6 (C016's geometric scorer reached 0.23). If go: use the CNN's mitosis score as a gate/feature in the C016 candidate stage (`division_scorer_stage.py`) and replay on 97 movies; expected value low (151 GT divisions, label noise per thread 742942).
- **(b) result 16:40 KST - NO-GO.** Crops: 151 GT divisions + 7,960 one-child GT nodes (40 per movie). 3D CNN, movie-grouped 5-fold OOF: precision 0.32 @ recall 0.54 (thr 0.3), 0.46 @ 0.40 (0.5), 0.62 @ 0.28 (0.7), 0.71 @ 0.16 (0.8); precision at recall 0.5 = 0.58 (bar was 0.6) - and that is on a 1:53 sample. In the pipeline the candidate parents outnumber recoverable divisions ~2000:1 (C016), so at thr 0.8 the CNN would keep ~6 false parents per movie for 0.4 true ones (precision ~6 %), and pruning the existing forks with it would drop half of the true divisions. Appearance alone does not separate mitosis in these light-sheet nuclei at the needed rate (label noise, 151 positives). Closed; model saved at `experiments/candidates/c031_division_cnn/division_cnn.pt` for the record.

**Outcome of section 24:** both attempts closed with measurements; nothing was submitted from this section and the final picks (section 23) are unchanged.

## 25. Independent second opinion (2026-09-26, user-requested review)

Report: `reports/second_opinion_20260926.md`. **Final picks remain C024 + C023. No new candidate, upload, kernel push or submission.** The conclusion that repeated parameter sweeps are exhausted is supported; the broader claim that every possible direction was experimentally ruled out is too strong. Future-aware structured re-assignment is proposed in the public audit but has no located C023 replay result; its external LB evidence remains weak.

**New inference hypothesis: fuse the two temporal contexts already encoded for each interior frame.** The actual C023 predictor uses 2-frame windows with stride 1 and `seen_frames`: frame t's detections and head refinement come from its first occurrence `(t-1,t)`, while the t detection map from `(t,t+1)` is discarded. The temporal UNet uses temporal attention. Using the second context is distinct from previously rejected spatial/edge-probability TTA, head averaging, or swapping the order of the same two frames. This is not a confirmed bug or gain.

- Small direct probe using the exact dual-seed D4 encode/blend block with retention guard, TF32 off, fixed t=25/50/75 in `44b6_12dfb391` and `6bba_05db0fb1`: past-context peaks 3,819, future-context 3,882, mean-logit-map peaks 3,856; 162 future peaks have no past peak within 1.7 um, and 99 past peaks have no future counterpart. Past-context counts equal cached C023 detections on all six frames. **These are candidate differences, not verified recovered nuclei or an e2e gain.** No head/link/ILP/PP scoring was performed for this hypothesis. Details: `reports/temporal_context_probe_20260926.json` + `.log`.
- Recommendation if continuing: one fixed detection-logit temporal-mean variant on C023, keeping the head/edge/PP settings fixed; implement through the existing predictor and verify the off path. Run fresh inference with `run_kaggle_predict_local.py` before existing official replay; cached head-v1 graphs cannot serve as C023 controls. Start with 12 movies, extend only if promising, and limit the initial investigation to one day. This is an untested proposal, not a queued run. No numerical LB gain is predicted.

**Fresh finalist residual audit, 12 movies:** re-executed each finalist notebook through the existing replay namespace with its own head-specific ILP graphs, lowdet dumps, DeepCenter and integer output coordinates. Every saved per-movie adjusted-edge value and edge/division confusion count reproduced exactly. C023 total 0.9546997382; C024 0.9593167349. Across 7,878 GT edges, C023 misses 252 (endpoint unmatched 90, both diverted 95, half break 66, pure break 1), C024 misses 243 (80/95/67/1). Of both-diverted misses, 89/86 have 1:1 structure and past/future neighbours, which only establishes possible targets for a future-aware diagnostic. Recovered-edge overlap: both 7,600, C023-only 26, C024-only 35, neither 217. This GT-informed union is not an achievable ensemble score. Details: `reports/finalist_residual_audit_20260926.json` + `.log`.

Scope correction: the previously quoted ~45/35/20 residual split directly comes from `reports/gap_headroom_c022.txt` (C022, **22** movies), not a C023/C024 97-movie measurement. All these local results remain in-sample at embryo level. No public/private improvement is established by this review.

## 41. Consolidated handoff and exact resume point (2026-09-29, observed 00:14 KST)

This section records the completed evidence and the running C054 job for the user's handoff request. It does not restart any experiment or change the scheduled follow-up.

### 41.1 Research decisions that led here

- The [whole-embryo split audit](state/split_audit_20260928/REVIEW.md) established pixel-identical source overlap between the old 24 training movies and evaluation-97. The 199 movies derive from two embryos (71 / 128 movies), not 199 independent biological samples. C047 was stopped before fitting and must not resume. C041–C046 pooled local improvements remain technical fit-domain evidence; their actual Kaggle results and execution-parity proofs remain valid.
- C048 corrected the appearance study using whole-embryo fits; results were small and mixed. C049 corrected the image-division learner, C050 corrected the separate candidate learner, and C051 tested boundary-support registration. None justified production integration. C046's exact T4 verification is retained, but it is not selected for submission. See the [C032–C051 closure review](state/research_closure_20260928/REVIEW.md) for individual dispositions.
- The [first-principles review](state/priority_review_20260928/REVIEW.md) identified missing actual division supervision in C037 as the strongest supported next experiment. C052 therefore reused production Transformer inputs and the existing known-label objective, adding actual division batches with whole-embryo folds.
- C053 converted the two retained C052 models into one fixed parameter mean for deployment. It retained some graph improvement but had 23 more edge FPs and 2 more division FPs than C052 opposite-fold evaluation. The renewed user request authorizes C054 to test a fixed output mean. This comparison is a hypothesis about combination behavior; it does not isolate the cause of C053's losses or create independent validation.

### 41.5 Completion review and conditional submission procedure

1. Verify all 14 jobs succeeded and independently validate every pinned input / recorded output hash. Preserve exact errors and completed artifacts on failure; repair only the failed portion. Do not edit pinned sources or rerun completed training / inference blindly.
2. Confirm the two full-movie controls, 97 unchanged detector outputs, and actual graph stages (ILP, motion, pre-restore, final). Review signed total / edge / division TP, FP and FN by embryo, heldout-12, confirm-10, extension-75 and all-97 against C023, C052 and C053. Probability changes alone do not justify promotion. C054 fixed-97 remains fit-domain evidence.
3. Require actual clean portable-12 replay, production writer-4, official visible-4 and `local_verification.json`. **Use C054's resulting full-precision score and hashes; never substitute C053's target.** Save a review / decision before deployment.
4. If worthwhile, upload the prepared private bundle `dataset/transformer_output_mean600.pt` and push only `biohub-c054-output-ensemble.ipynb`, intended dataset and notebook ref **`taeyangg4/biohub-c054-output-ensemble`**. Local `audit_*.ipynb` files must not be pushed. Reuse the existing C053 remote CLI / SDK procedure; no progress-check repush.
5. On actual T4 completion, establish canonical SDK version identity **before** using CLI output retrieval, which ignores the requested version and fetches latest. Collect that exact version's source and outputs once. Normalize notebook cell source string/list representations; compare source, model bundle and both member-load logs, predictor/head/runtime, CSV byte hash, official full-precision visible-4 score, actual Tesla T4 / FP32 / math SDPA and all four movies' `repair_fallback=0` / `deadline_degraded=0` against local proof. Equal scores alone are insufficient.
6. Immediately before submission, read fresh shared 5-per-UTC-day quota and check existing submissions / identical outputs. Submit only the exact verified version under existing authorization. Record the accepted submission ID, version, hash and response in the ledger and durable action immediately. On quota error or uncertain acceptance, read back before any retry.
7. A new finite remote phase needs its own grounded ETA and the same ETA-minus-10 / 20-minute heartbeat. If no worthwhile candidate or evidence-backed next action remains, save the decision and delete the heartbeat. Preserve C023/C024 and report no unverified leaderboard improvement.

## 42. User-linked Lineage Forge V12 source review (2026-09-29)

The user supplied `flexonafft/biohub-lineage-forge-precision-tracking` as a newly shared notebook. Existing radar data was read first, then one targeted live metadata request and source pull confirmed **V12 / run 353597016**, updated Sep 28 21:57 KST and completed on T4. The displayed best **0.947 is V8**, not a measured V12 improvement; linked current submission `56644638` has no score in the returned metadata. No score polling was started.

Only code cells 0/4/5 differ from our saved reference. V12 adds **guarded endpoint readmission** of observed pooled detections at >=0.94, with <=3 um steps, <=1.5 um motion residual, candidate-ambiguity rejection, bridge preference, and a min(100, 0.2% of nodes) addition budget. C023 already uses readmission at >=0.965 within 4 um followed by stabilized relinking, so the motion / ambiguity / budget guards are the useful distinct component. V12 also changes ILP division weight 1.2->0.4 and DeepCenter division threshold .20->.25; do not import those settings as proven improvements. Prior .7/.5 ILP division experiments had poor results.

Actual public run artifacts select `tight55` on the familiar eight validation movies: proxy .9479624763164339 -> .950017415688161, divisions 3 TP / 1 FP / 9 FN. This is a postprocessing-selection result with **no readmission-off control**, not isolated evidence for the new stage or independent validation. The public source lacks our V1284 head and is not a replacement for the scored C023 stack.

Decision: retain guarded endpoint readmission as a **possible later fixed-component replay**, using existing caches / official harness after cache semantics and off-control parity are checked. No experiment or Kaggle write was launched. C054 remains the active study with its existing schedule and immutable sources. Full source diff, hashes, limitations and conditional follow-up are in [state/public_lineage_review_20260929/REVIEW.md](state/public_lineage_review_20260929/REVIEW.md).
