**C055 (user-directed, section 42 follow-up).** Only V12's motion-predicted endpoint readmission, replacing C023's readmission, replayed with the pinned harness on the existing C023 FP32 caches: `src/c055_guarded_readmit.py`, `src/c055_guarded_readmit_stage.py`, `experiments/candidates/c055_guarded_readmit/`, `state/c055_continuation.md`. Cache semantics proven on all 97 movies (threshold-independent local-max mask, sigmoid scores, [1,4,4] scaling, production count == peaks > 0.965, dedup no-op); head-refined nodes sit up to 1.64 um from their raw peaks, so existing nodes' own peaks are excluded by one-to-one identity before V12's 1.5 um separation test (the single documented adaptation). Arms control / readmit_off (diagnostic) / v12_guarded; control parity is exact against the stored 2026-09-26 rows, the harness CLI and C046's `off` final graphs. First launch failed in the cache check (two nodes sharing a nearest peak; archived in `state/c055_failed_launch_20260929/`), second launch 02:36 KST. Early heldout12: readmit_off +0.0024 and v12_guarded +0.0024 vs control, v12 vs readmit_off -0.00006, 561 nodes added / 532 surviving — the same local readmit-off pattern that C015 showed and that lost 0.001 on the LB (section 18), so V12's own contribution must be judged as v12_guarded minus readmit_off. Results and the proceed / hold decision go to the study's `REVIEW.md` (section 44 when written).

**C055 22-movie result and the missed-node diagnostic (2026-09-29 03:50 KST).** all22: control 0.945986; readmit_off +0.001910 (edge +0.001335); v12_guarded +0.001553 (edge +0.000979), i.e. V12's own contribution is v12_guarded − readmit_off = **−0.000357** (20/22 movies −0.0001…−0.0004 through the linear node term `1 − 0.1·(t_pred − t_true)/t_true` with raw edge Jaccard unchanged; 2 movies +1 TP each; 6bba_474be664 −0.0139 with TP −2 / FP +3). 923 nodes added, 874 survive to the final graph, 931 edges survive; they recover almost no annotated edge. Read-only diagnostic `state/c055_diagnostics_20260929/` on the 213 GT nodes the control misses (379 GT edges): **101 have no primary-detector peak ≥ 0.3 within 3 µm; 91 have a production peak (> 0.965) that the pipeline dropped — 63 never selected by the ILP (107 GT edges), 28 linked in the ILP output (degree 1–2) but deleted before restore, i.e. by the short-track filter (48 GT edges); only 6 lie in V12's 0.94–0.965 range; 15 in lower ranges.** So V12's candidate range cannot pay for its node-term cost; the recoverable pools are detector recall (101) and pipeline node selection (91, a node-budget lever closed in section 21 for uniform rules). C055 extension75 was still running at this writing (the gate passed only through the readmit-off component); final numbers go to its REVIEW.md.

**C056 launched 03:59 KST (PID 57480, `experiments/candidates/c056_complementary_readmit/`, 15,353 pinned inputs).** Same V12 guard, insertion point and replay protocol as C055, but candidates come from the retained Hengck StrongUNet detector (`src/cache_strongunet_gpu_peaks.py` model, fixed published p ≥ 0.5; 2.5 s per movie on the 4070 Ti SUPER, ~65k peaks at p ≥ 0.5 per 44b6 movie) cached from `data/train` zarr for the 22 (then 75) movies; `src/c056_complementary_readmit.py prepare|run|cache_peaks|diagnose|study|analyse`, `src/c056_complementary_readmit_stage.py`. Arms control / readmit_off (must equal C055 rows exactly) / strongunet_guarded; `diagnose.json` first reports the ceiling (missed GT nodes with a StrongUNet p ≥ 0.5 peak within 3 µm). Pre-registered gate: strongunet_guarded must beat BOTH the control and readmit_off on all22 total and edge, both embryos ≥ 0 vs control, survivors > 0. No fitting, sweep or Kaggle write.

**Complementary-detector ceiling versus burden (2026-09-29 04:30 KST, `state/c055_diagnostics_20260929/strongunet_burden_ceiling_summary.csv`, 22 movies).** StrongUNet peaks that are complementary (> 1.7 µm from every primary peak ≥ 0.3 and every C023 final node): p ≥ 0.5 → 196,198 peaks (37.5 % of the final node count) covering 40 of the 213 missed GT nodes (73 of 379 missed GT edges); p ≥ 0.7 → 135,026 (23.6 %), 32 nodes / 60 edges; p ≥ 0.8 → 71,267 (11.3 %), 11 / 20; p ≥ 0.9 → 10,615 (1.5 %), 0; p ≥ 0.95 → 227, 0. Under the linear node term an ILP-level candidate union would cost roughly −0.011 (p ≥ 0.8) to −0.038 (p ≥ 0.5) for at most +0.004 of edge Jaccard, so bulk admission of the second detector is closed locally; the covering peaks are low-confidence and indistinguishable from the tens of thousands of other complementary peaks without GT. C056's guarded variant on heldout12 admitted 55 of 169,690 candidates, recovered no annotated edge and lost 2 TP on 44b6_2a2eff9f (−0.00072 vs readmit_off).

**Division term arithmetic (2026-09-29 03:35 KST, from the 2026-09-28 division audit and the C054 97-movie counts).** 149 of the 151 annotated parents and 268 of the 302 annotated daughters exist as nodes in C023's final graphs, but only 27 forks score true (27 TP / 46 FP / 124 FN, micro Jaccard 0.137): the missed divisions are a fork-linking problem, not detection. Because the term is micro-averaged and far from saturation, one recovered fork is worth ≈ +0.0005 of the 97-movie total and one false fork ≈ −0.00007 on the division term plus ≈ −0.00006 on the size-weighted edge term (its false edge), so a fork source breaks even near **precision 0.2–0.3** and gives ≥ +0.005 only from precision ≳ 0.3 at recall ≳ 0.3. The closed sources sat at exactly that bar (C049 cross-embryo image CNN 0.16 / 0.25 precision at recall 0.5; C050 candidate learner 0.04 / 0.06; ILP fork re-injection and C039's public division rule far below), which is why every variant lost or tied. Reopen divisions only with a candidate scorer that clears ~0.3 precision on whole-embryo opposite folds; no such signal is available tonight.

**C055 and C056 closed (2026-09-29 03:50 KST).** C055 97 movies (all parity exact, 316 artifacts, manifest `f86e73057c7a3fa2…`): v12_guarded +0.000260 total / +0.000190 edge vs C023 on all97, but **−0.000051 on extension75 and −0.002840 on 44b6 (27 movies)**; readmit_off +0.000431 all97 and **−0.002695 on 44b6** (edge TP −21 / FP +8); v12 − readmit_off negative on 72/75 extension movies (raw edge Jaccard changed on 7); 3,420 nodes added, 3,209 surviving, recovering TP +2. C056 (StrongUNet p ≥ 0.5 candidates through the same guard) failed its 22-movie gate: −0.000339 vs readmit_off, −0.001573 vs control on 44b6, 56 nodes added, no annotated edge recovered. **Decision: hold both; no portable / T4 / submission; C023 / C024 0.954 final picks unchanged.** Endpoint readmission under a motion guard cannot recover annotated edges from either candidate source, and the readmit-off component flips sign by embryo (a node-budget lever, section 21; C015 LB −0.001).
